It connects the things you already read and watch with vocabulary, dictionary, writing and review tools.<\/p><\/details>\n        <details><summary>What can I learn with Ribble?<svg class=\"icon\"><use href=\"#i-chev\"/><\/svg><\/summary><p>You can learn from books, documents, videos and YouTube, then save vocabulary and practise it through writing, quizzes and spaced repetition.<\/p><\/details>\n        <details><summary>Does Ribble include a free library?<svg class=\"icon\"><use href=\"#i-chev\"/><\/svg><\/summary><p>Yes. Every account includes a free vocabulary library organized by language and level, with ready-made topic decks.<\/p><\/details>\n        <details><summary>How does the dictionary work?<svg class=\"icon\"><use href=\"#i-chev\"/><\/svg><\/summary><p>Look up any word to see pronunciation, grammar, translations, contextual examples, synonyms and antonyms, then save it directly to your deck.<\/p><\/details>\n        <details><summary>How does Ribble help with writing?<svg class=\"icon\"><use href=\"#i-chev\"/><\/svg><\/summary><p>The writing assistant scores your attempt, highlights mistakes, provides a corrected version and explains each change in plain language.<\/p><\/details>\n        <details><summary>What is spaced repetition?<svg class=\"icon\"><use href=\"#i-chev\"/><\/svg><\/summary><p>It is a review system that resurfaces a card just before you are likely to forget it. Your Again, Hard, Good or Easy rating adjusts the schedule.<\/p><\/details>\n      <\/div>\n    <\/div>\n  <\/section>\n\n  <section class=\"sec\" id=\"start\">\n    <div class=\"start\">\n      <div <script>
(function(){
  var stage=document.getElementById('stage'),list=document.getElementById('list'),wrap=stage.parentNode;
  var items=[].slice.call(list.children),cards=[].slice.call(stage.children);
  var mq=matchMedia('(max-width:760px)'),act=0,sls=[],dots=[],tick=0;
  cards.forEach(function(c,i){
    var li=items[i],sl=document.createElement('div');
    sl.className='sl'+(i===0?' on':'');
    stage.insertBefore(sl,c);sl.appendChild(c);
    var info=document.createElement('div');info.className='sl-info';
    info.innerHTML='<div class="r"><span class="sl-no"></span><span class="sl-nm"></span></div><span class="sl-tx"></span><div class="chips"></div>';
    info.querySelector('.sl-no').textContent=li.querySelector('.no').textContent;
    info.querySelector('.sl-nm').textContent=li.querySelector('.nm').textContent;
    info.querySelector('.sl-tx').textContent=li.querySelector('.tx').textContent;
    info.querySelector('.chips').innerHTML=li.querySelector('.chips').innerHTML;
    sl.appendChild(info);sls.push(sl);
  });
  var dw=document.createElement('div');dw.className='sl-dots';
  sls.forEach(function(s,i){var d=document.createElement('i');if(i===0)d.className='on';d.addEventListener('click',function(){go(i)});dw.appendChild(d);dots.push(d)});
  wrap.appendChild(dw);
  function go(i){stage.scrollTo({left:sls[i].offsetLeft-12,behavior:'smooth'})}
  function sync(){
    tick=0;if(!mq.matches||sls.length<2)return;
    var step=sls[1].offsetLeft-sls[0].offsetLeft;if(!step)return;
    var i=Math.max(0,Math.min(sls.length-1,Math.round(stage.scrollLeft/step)));
    if(i===act)return;act=i;
    sls.forEach(function(s,j){s.classList.toggle('on',j===i);dots[j].classList.toggle('on',j===i)});
    items[i].firstChild.click();
  }
  stage.addEventListener('scroll',function(){if(!tick)tick=requestAnimationFrame(sync)},{passive:true});
})();
</script>

<script id="pn-open-js">
(function(){
  var stage=document.getElementById('stage'),list=document.getElementById('list');
  var items=[].slice.call(list.children),cards=[].slice.call(stage.querySelectorAll('.card'));
  function buildApp(card){
    var scr=card.querySelector('.scr');
    var links=[].map.call(document.querySelectorAll('link[rel=stylesheet],link[rel=preconnect]'),function(l){return l.outerHTML}).join('');
    var css=[].map.call(document.querySelectorAll('style'),function(s){return s.outerHTML}).join('');
    return '<!DOCTYPE html><html><head><meta charset="utf-8"><meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width,initial-scale=1">'+links+css+
      '<style>html,body{margin:0;height:auto!important;overflow:auto!important;background:#fbfaf8!important}.scr{position:relative!important;inset:auto!important;border-radius:0!important;width:max(100vw,900px)!important;height:max(100vh,620px)!important;margin:0!important}.scr.app{container-type:inline-size}</style></head><body>'+scr.outerHTML+'</body></html>';
  }
  function open(i){
    var card=cards[i],f=card.querySelector('.site');
    var html=f?f.srcdoc:buildApp(card);
    var url=(card.querySelector('.url')||{}).textContent||'';
    var title=items[i].querySelector('.nm').textContent;
    try{parent.postMessage({pnOpen:1,title:title,url:url,html:html},'*')}catch(e){}
  }
  cards.forEach(function(card,i){
    var b=document.createElement('button');b.type='button';b.className='pn-open';
    b.setAttribute('aria-label','Open '+items[i].querySelector('.nm').textContent+' full screen');
    b.innerHTML='Open preview <svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M8 16 16 8M9 8h7v7"/></svg>';
    card.appendChild(b);
    b.addEventListener('click',function(e){e.stopPropagation();open(i)});
    card.addEventListener('click',function(){open(i)});
    var f=card.querySelector('.site');
    if(f){
      var hook=function(){try{var d=f.contentDocument;if(!d||!d.documentElement||d.__pn)return;d.__pn=1;
        d.addEventListener('click',function(e){e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();open(i)},true)}catch(x){}};
      f.addEventListener('load',hook);[300,1200,3000].forEach(function(t){setTimeout(hook,t)});hook();
    }
  });
  items.forEach(function(li,i){
    li.addEventListener('click',function(e){if(e.isTrusted&&li.classList.contains('on'))open(i)},true);
  });
  [].slice.call(stage.querySelectorAll('.sl-info')).forEach(function(inf,i){inf.addEventListener('click',function(){open(i)})});
})();
</script>

<script id="phone-cover-js">
(function(){
  var stage=document.getElementById('stage');if(!stage)return;
  var sls=[].slice.call(stage.querySelectorAll('.sl'));if(!sls.length)return;
  var lk=document.createElement('link');lk.rel='stylesheet';lk.href='https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Playfair+Display:wght@700&display=swap';document.head.appendChild(lk);
  var page=document.querySelector('.page'),hd=document.createElement('div');
  hd.className='sc-head';hd.innerHTML='<h2>Selected work</h2>';page.insertBefore(hd,page.firstChild);
  function load(ph){var f=ph.querySelector('iframe');if(f&&ph._src&&!f.getAttribute('srcdoc')){f.setAttribute('scrolling','no');f.srcdoc=ph._src}}
  var io=window.IntersectionObserver?new IntersectionObserver(function(es){es.forEach(function(e){if(e.isIntersecting){load(e.target);io.unobserve(e.target)}})},{root:stage,rootMargin:'0px 100% 0px 100%'}):null;
  function open(sl){var b=sl.querySelector('.pn-open');if(b)b.click()}
  sls.forEach(function(sl){
    var site=sl.querySelector('.site'),name=(sl.querySelector('.sl-nm')||{}).textContent||'project';
    var ph=document.createElement('div');ph.className='sc-ph';
    ph.innerHTML='<div class="sc-pf"><iframe tabindex="-1" aria-hidden="true" title="" scrolling="no"></iframe></div><i class="sc-gl"></i><i class="sc-isl"></i><i class="sc-hb"></i>';
    ph._src=site?site.srcdoc:'';sl.insertBefore(ph,sl.firstChild);
    ['a','b','c','d'].forEach(function(c){var i=document.createElement('i');i.className='sc-sb '+c;sl.appendChild(i)});
    var op=document.createElement('button');op.type='button';op.className='sc-open';
    op.setAttribute('aria-label','Open '+name+' preview');
    op.innerHTML='Open preview <svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M7 17 17 7M8 7h9v9"/></svg>';
    op.addEventListener('click',function(e){e.stopPropagation();open(sl)});
    sl.appendChild(op);
    sl.addEventListener('click',function(){open(sl)});
    if(io)io.observe(ph);else load(ph);
  });
  function fit(){sls.forEach(function(sl){var pf=sl.querySelector('.sc-pf'),f=pf&&pf.querySelector('iframe');if(!f)return;var w=pf.clientWidth,h=pf.clientHeight;if(!w)return;var k=w/390;f.style.width='390px';f.style.height=(h/k)+'px';f.style.transform='scale('+k+')'})}
  fit();addEventListener('resize',fit);setTimeout(fit,400);
})();
</script>

<script id="phone-polish-js">
(function(){
  var stage=document.getElementById('stage');if(!stage)return;
  var mq=matchMedia('(max-width:760px)'),sls=[].slice.call(stage.querySelectorAll('.sl')),raf=0;
  if(!sls.length)return;
  sls.forEach(function(s,i){s.style.setProperty('--i',i)});
  function upd(){
    raf=0;
    if(!mq.matches){sls.forEach(function(s){s.style.removeProperty('--d')});return}
    var c=stage.clientWidth/2,step=sls.length>1?(sls[1].offsetLeft-sls[0].offsetLeft):300;
    sls.forEach(function(s){
      var m=s.offsetLeft+s.offsetWidth/2-stage.scrollLeft;
      s.style.setProperty('--d',Math.min(1,Math.abs(m-c)/step).toFixed(3));
    });
  }
  stage.addEventListener('scroll',function(){if(!raf)raf=requestAnimationFrame(upd)},{passive:true});
  addEventListener('resize',upd);upd();setTimeout(upd,250);setTimeout(upd,900);
  var dw=document.querySelector('.sl-dots');
  if(dw)dw.addEventListener('click',function(e){
    var d=e.target.closest&&e.target.closest('i');if(!d)return;
    e.stopPropagation();
    var i=[].indexOf.call(dw.children,d),step=sls[1].offsetLeft-sls[0].offsetLeft;
    stage.scrollTo({left:i*step,behavior:'smooth'});
  },true);
  var arrow='<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M7 17 17 7M8 7h9v9"/></svg>';
  sls.forEach(function(sl){
    var op=sl.querySelector('.sc-open'),inf=sl.querySelector('.sl-info');
    if(op&&inf){op.innerHTML=arrow;inf.appendChild(op)}
    var f=sl.querySelector('.sc-pf iframe');
    if(f)f.addEventListener('load',function(){if(f.getAttribute('srcdoc'))setTimeout(function(){f.classList.add('rdy')},80)});
  });
  var n=0,t=setInterval(function(){
    sls.forEach(function(sl){
      var f=sl.querySelector('.sc-pf iframe');
      if(f&&f.getAttribute('srcdoc')&&!f.classList.contains('rdy')){
        try{if(f.contentDocument&&f.contentDocument.readyState==='complete')f.classList.add('rdy')}catch(e){}
      }
    });
    if(++n>60)clearInterval(t);
  },500);
})();
</script>

<script id="phone-pro-js">
(function(){
  var stage=document.getElementById('stage');if(!stage)return;
  var sls=[].slice.call(stage.querySelectorAll('.sl')),mq=matchMedia('(max-width:760px)'),raf=0;
  function upd(){raf=0;if(!mq.matches)return;var c=stage.scrollLeft+stage.clientWidth/2;sls.forEach(function(s){var m=s.offsetLeft+s.offsetWidth/2,p=Math.max(-1,Math.min(1,(m-c)/s.offsetWidth));s.style.setProperty('--p',p.toFixed(3))})}
  stage.addEventListener('scroll',function(){if(!raf)raf=requestAnimationFrame(upd)},{passive:true});
  addEventListener('resize',upd);upd();setTimeout(upd,300);setTimeout(upd,1000);
  var h=document.createElement('div');h.className='sw-hint';
  h.innerHTML='<span>Swipe</span><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M5 12h14M13 6l6 6-6 6"/></svg>';
  stage.parentNode.appendChild(h);
  var gone=0;stage.addEventListener('scroll',function(){if(!gone&&stage.scrollLeft>20){gone=1;h.classList.add('off')}},{passive:true});
})();
